# Wiki Index

**GrokVault Content Catalog**

This is the primary navigation aid for the wiki. It is maintained by Grok. Read this file first when exploring or before answering questions.

Last updated: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *The World as Will and Representation*, Volume 2 (Arthur Schopenhauer, 1844; Payne 1958 / Dover 1966)** — The 1844 supplements, not a second system. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 9 reading subagents. Created 7 pages: source-summary [[Sources/The World as Will and Representation Volume 2 - Schopenhauer (Payne, Dover 2012)]] and 6 concepts (Theory of the Ludicrous; Man’s Need for Metaphysics; Primacy of the Will in Self-Consciousness; Genius; History as Particular; Metaphysics of Sexual Love). Primary-rewrote *Parerga* II pages for Indestructibility, Affirmation/Denial, Vanity, and Religion-as-allegory’s *sensu stricto* cut. Will is nearest, not naked. Worst of all possible worlds now sourced to Ch. XLVI. Inborn error: we exist to be happy (Ch. XLIX). He still does not name the system “pessimism.” MOBI + .md filed to `raw/Ingested/`. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *On Vision and Colours* (Arthur Schopenhauer, 1816 / 1854; Cartwright/Erdmann/Janaway, Cambridge 2012)** — Colour is the qualitatively divided activity of the retina. Goethe delivered the data; this treatise the theory. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 3 reading subagents. Created 2 pages: source-summary [[Sources/On Vision and Colours - Schopenhauer (Cambridge 2012)]], [[Concepts/Physiological Theory of Colour (Schopenhauer)]]. Ch. I is the first rehearsal of FR §21 (palette-blobs, stereoscope, illusion vs error). Kantian primer. No Goethe/Newton thinker page. Same EPUB as Fourfold Root; *On Will in Nature* still outstanding. Stahl/Runge 2010 stays in `raw/`. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *The Dawn of Day* (Friedrich Nietzsche, 1881; 1886 Preface; J. M. Kennedy)** — The missing free-spirit middle between HATH I–III and *The Gay Science*. Extracted from the 2013 22-work collection. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 6 reading subagents. Created 4 pages: source-summary [[Sources/The Dawn of Day - Nietzsche (Kennedy, Collection 2013)]] and 3 concepts ([[Concepts/Morality of Custom (Nietzsche)]], [[Concepts/Faith in Morals (Nietzsche)]], [[Concepts/Feeling of Power (Nietzsche)]]). 1886 Preface: undermine faith in morals; Circe of philosophers; *autosuppression of morals*. 1881: custom *is* morality (§9); deny morals as alchemy (§103); feeling/love/demon of power — **not** will to power; pity multiplies suffering; §575 other birds will fly farther. Absent: WzP, death of God, *amor fati*, recurrence, ressentiment, master/slave. Collection EPUB stays in `raw/`. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *Parerga and Paralipomena*, Volume 2 (Arthur Schopenhauer, 1851; Payne, Oxford 1974/2000)** — The *paralipomena*: thirty-one short essays for a second visit. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 8 reading subagents. Created 8 pages: source-summary [[Sources/Parerga and Paralipomena Volume 2 - Schopenhauer (Payne 2000)]] and 7 concepts (Compassion and Justice; Indestructibility of Our True Nature; Vanity and Suffering of Existence; Affirmation and Denial of the Will-to-Live; Religion as Allegory; Thinking for Oneself; On Women). Suicide ≠ denial. Religion true at best *sensu allegorico*. “On Women” filed as rant, not as vault fact. He still does not call his philosophy “pessimism.” *WWR* II MOBI stays in `raw/`. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *On the Fourfold Root of the Principle of Sufficient Reason* (Arthur Schopenhauer, 1813 / 1847; Cartwright/Erdmann/Janaway, Cambridge 2012)** — The propaedeutic *WWR* demanded. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 4 reading subagents. Created 3 pages: source-summary [[Sources/On the Fourfold Root of the Principle of Sufficient Reason - Schopenhauer (Cambridge 2012)]], [[Concepts/Fourfold Root of the Principle of Sufficient Reason (Schopenhauer)]], [[Concepts/Intellectual Nature of Intuition (Schopenhauer)]]. 1847 ≠ 1813. PSR is four a priori laws, not a denial of reasons. Existing Leibniz contradiction's "No Sufficient Reason" bullet rewritten. Volume also contains *On Vision and Colours* and *On Will in Nature* — **not** this ingest; EPUB stays in `raw/`. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *Parerga and Paralipomena*, Volume 1 (Arthur Schopenhauer, 1851; Roehr/Janaway, Cambridge 2014)** — First Schopenhauer primary after *WWR* I, and the book that made him famous. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 7 reading subagents. Created 7 pages: source-summary [[Sources/Parerga and Paralipomena Volume 1 - Schopenhauer (Cambridge 2014)]] and 6 concepts (Doctrine of the Ideal and the Real; University Philosophy; Transcendent Fatalism; Spirit-Seeing and Idealistic Explanation; Eudaemonology as Accommodation; What One Is, Has, and Represents). He claims to have solved Descartes’ ideal/real problem (will = the real); Fichte/Schelling/Hegel are sophists; university philosophy is philosophy for hire; fate essay is labeled fantasy (*amor fati* absent); Aphorisms are an **accommodation** (eudaemonology is a euphemism). He does not call his philosophy “pessimism.” Volume 2 of *Parerga* and *WWR* II (MOBI in `raw/`) remain out. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *Ecce Homo: How One Becomes What One Is* (Friedrich Nietzsche, written 1888; published 1908; 2013 22-work collection)** — Nietzsche's last completed book and the one that tells the vault how to read the other thirteen. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 6 parallel reading subagents. Created 7 pages: source-summary [[Sources/Ecce Homo - Nietzsche (Collection 2013)]] and 6 concepts (The Small Things; Becoming What One Is; Style as Communication of a State; Inspiration as Revelation; The Immoralist; Revaluation of All Values). Updated [[Thinkers/Nietzsche]], [[Concepts/Amor Fati (Love of Fate)]] (the "formula for greatness" is now **primary**, and its contrast term is *idealism*, not Stoic resignation), [[Concepts/Eternal Recurrence (Nietzsche)]] (August 1881, Silvaplana, "six thousand feet beyond man and time"), Great Health, Decadence, Genealogy, Ascetic Ideal, Free Spirit, Superman, Redemption of the It Was, Philosophizing with a Hammer, Apollinian and Dionysian, and the *Zarathustra* and *HATH* source pages. The corpus divides into a Yes-half and a No-half; all post-*Zarathustra* books are "bait." **This edition is missing the Preface and "Why I am So Wise."** **Do not cite this book for the will to power** (twice, never as doctrine) or for a four-part *Revaluation*. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of *Visions of Politics, Volume 1: Regarding Method* (Quentin Skinner, 2002)** — The Cambridge School's methodological charter, and the first thing in the vault that contests *how* intellectual history should be done. Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 6 parallel reading subagents. Created 9 pages: source-summary [[Sources/Visions of Politics Volume 1 - Regarding Method - Quentin Skinner (2002)]], thinkers [[Thinkers/Quentin Skinner]] / [[Thinkers/J.L. Austin]], 6 concepts (Mythologies in the History of Ideas; Illocutionary Force and Historical Meaning; Explaining Beliefs Without Truth; Innovating Ideologist and Legitimation; Rhetorical Redescription and Paradiastole; Cultural Lexicon - Words vs Concepts). Updated Lovejoy, Butterfield, Foucault archaeology, Wittgenstein language-games. Texts are acts: recover what an author was **doing** in writing. Reading the canon for "dateless wisdom" yields "not histories but more appropriately mythologies." Lovejoy's unit-ideas are the named target — a real contradiction, flagged both sides, not reconciled. **Ch. 10 withdraws a premise chs. 4 and 8 rest on**; cite the mythologies, not the fixed-lexicon picture. Skinner denies he ever attacked Begriffsgeschichte. *Liberty before Liberalism* (the Berlin engagement) is NOT in this volume. See [[log]].

Ventromedial patients know but cannot feel the future; feeling is body-landscape perception; the named error is the *Discourse* slogan's afterlife, not Meditation VI's union. Spinoza unnamed. Fingerprints vs Clark/Barrett flagged, not flattened. See [[log]].

Large-Volume Protocol, orchestrator + 8 reading subagents. Created 8 pages: source-summary [[Sources/The Mystical Theology and The Divine Names - Dionysius the Areopagite]], [[Thinkers/Pseudo-Dionysius]], 6 concepts (Super-Essential Godhead; Cataphatic and Apophatic Theology; Divine Names; Divine Darkness and Unknowing; Ecstasy of Divine Love; Hierarchy of Existence, Life, and Intelligence). Primary rewrite of the Christian dress of Athenian Neoplatonism: names as emanations, then stripped in Moses' Darkness; DN IV.10 is Lovejoy's plenitude line; matter is not evil (IV.28); God is both Eternity and Time and before both (X.3). Rolt's Bradley-Absolute reading flagged as interpolation. *Hierarchies* still outstanding. See [[log]].

Ziporyn: composite commonplace book; *dao* = course (ironic nondoing); *de* = unlearned virtuosity; A/B reversal occupies the unhewn. Updated Sun Tzu (resonance now grounded; strategic *tao* ≠ course), Heraclitus (unity of opposites vs. occupation of B), Maimonides (via negativa vs. generative nameless). *Zhuangzi* and the *Analects* remain outstanding. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-08-12 — **Ingest of "What Mary Didn't Know" (Frank Jackson, 1986)** — Brings the author of the knowledge argument into the vault as a primary voice rather than a reported opponent. Short-source protocol. Created 5 pages: source-summary [[Sources/What Mary Didn't Know - Frank Jackson (1986)]], [[Thinkers/Frank Jackson]], [[Arguments/Knowledge Argument (Jackson)]], [[Concepts/Physicalism as Complete Physical Knowledge (Jackson)]], [[Concepts/Ability Analysis of Phenomenal Knowledge (Lewis-Nemirow)]]. Corrects three misreadings the vault had absorbed second-hand: the argument is not about imagination, not about the intensionality of knowledge, and not about Mary's own experiences — the missing facts are about **other people**. Churchland's Leibniz's-Law reconstruction is disowned by Jackson; the Lewis–Nemirow ability analysis is answered by the other-minds-skepticism case; Jackson disclaims proof. Physicalism defined by the *W(P)* comparison (complete physical knowledge = complete knowledge simpliciter), the primary-source origin of Chalmers's "minimal physical duplicate." Updated Qualia Disqualified (the four-feature package is Dennett's reconstruction, not Jackson's claim), Zombie Argument, Naturalistic Dualism, Logical Supervenience. Ran concurrently with a separate ingest of "Epiphenomenal Qualia" (1982); the Jackson thinker and argument pages are shared and cover both papers. See [[log]].

Prior: 2026-07-01 — **Ingest of *Neoplatonism* (R. T. Wallis, 1972)** — The standard single-author survey of the whole Neoplatonic movement, Plotinus (204–70) to the closing of the Academy (529) and its afterlife. Frame: three interlocking aims (systematic metaphysics of the One; the soul's salvific return; defense of the Hellenic tradition against Christianity), with all later development read as answers to the problems Plotinus bequeathed (Wallis pp. 90–93). Prior: 2026-07-01 — **Ingest of Aristotle's complete *Nicomachean Ethics* (W.D. Ross) and the (Pseudo-)Aristotelian *Economics*** — Large-Volume Protocol for the *Ethics* (orchestrator + 5 parallel reading subagents, one per book-pair: I–II, III–IV, V–VI, VII–VIII, IX–X), short-source protocol for *Economics*. The *Ethics*: eudaimonia as activity in accordance with virtue, completed by contemplation (*theōria*) as the highest happiness in Book X; the doctrine of the mean instantiated across the particular virtues (courage, temperance, liberality, magnanimity); justice (distributive/corrective/equity); practical wisdom (*phronēsis*) as the mean's epistemic engine; akrasia resolved via the having/using distinction; friendship (*philia*) as an extension of proper self-love, necessary even for the self-sufficient. Explicitly hands off to the *Politics*. Created 10 pages: 2 source-summaries ([[Sources/Aristotle - Nicomachean Ethics (Complete, W.D. Ross)]], [[Sources/Aristotle (Pseudo) - Economics]]) + 8 concepts ([[Concepts/Doctrine of the Mean (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Voluntary Action and Choice (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Moral Virtues as Means (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Justice - Universal, Particular, and Equity (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Practical Wisdom - Phronesis (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Akrasia - Weakness of Will (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Friendship - Philia (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Contemplation and the Best Life - Theoria (Aristotle)]]). The *Economics*: household management as prior to statecraft, ranked wealth-getting modes, and an amoral catalogue of state fiscal stratagems — 2 concepts ([[Concepts/Household Management and the Ranking of Wealth-Getting (Pseudo-Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Revenue-Raising Stratagems (Pseudo-Aristotle Economics)]]). Updated [[Thinkers/Aristotle]] (new full-NE and Economics sections, now 9 primary sources), [[Concepts/Eudaimonia]] (completed with Book X), [[Concepts/Distributive Justice and the Rule of Law (Aristotle)]] (cross-linked to NE Book V), and marked [[Sources/Aristotle - Nicomachean Ethics (Book I)]] superseded. Stats: +12 pages → 656.

Prior: 2026-07-01 — **Ingest of Aristotle's *Parva Naturalia*: On Sleep and Waking, On Dreams, On Memory and Recollection, On Divination in Sleep** — Four short treatises continuing *De Anima*'s psychology into physiology. Sleep as privation of waking seated in the primary/common sense-organ (heart), caused by nutriment evaporation/reflux; dreaming as *phantasia*'s residual sense-movements amplified by sleep's quiet; memory as a seal-impression contemplated as a likeness of the absent past, with recollection as associative search (similarity/contrast/contiguity) exclusive to deliberative animals — founding classical associationism; divination in sleep given a naturalistic cause/token/coincidence taxonomy, denying divine sending and rejecting Democritus. Created 8 pages: 4 source-summaries ([[Sources/Aristotle - On Sleep and Waking]], [[Sources/Aristotle - On Dreams]], [[Sources/Aristotle - On Memory and Recollection]], [[Sources/Aristotle - On Divination in Sleep]]) + 4 concepts ([[Concepts/Sleep and Waking (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Dreams as Residual Sense-Movements (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Memory and Recollection (Aristotle)]], [[Concepts/Divination in Sleep (Aristotle)]]). Updated [[Thinkers/Aristotle]] (new Parva Naturalia section, now 7 primary sources). Opens a forward link to [[Thinkers/Hume]] for associationism. Follows same-day ingest of *On the Soul* (De Anima) and *On the Universe* (Pseudo-Aristotle, spurious). Stats: +8 pages → 644.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Physics of the Future* (Michio Kaku, 2011)** — physics-grounded forecast (4 forces + quantum theory; 300+ scientist interviews, prototypes exist) for 2100: Type 0 (fossil) → Type I planetary civilization (~100 years at 1–2% growth per Kardashev scale; control planetary energy/weather, internet as Type I phone, democracy spread, diseases planetary, "A Day in the Life in 2100" with mind interfaces/abundance). Ch1 "Mind over Matter" (BCI/thought control via room-temp superconductors, "age of magnetism"; brain neural net/Hebb/parallel vs. digital). Ch2 "Future of AI: Rise of the Machines" (narrow progress but limits: pattern recognition/common sense unsolved 50+ years; ASIMO "insect IQ" scripted; Deep Blue brute force ≠ intelligence; AI winters; expert timelines 20–1000 years; Moore slowing post-2020–25 post-silicon). Medicine/nanotech/energy (stars/fusion/SSP)/space (to stars)/wealth/humanity (Type II/III, von Neumann probes, entropy). Measured optimism (hype vs. lab reality; resistance from terrorists/dictatorships). +1 source [[Sources/Physics of the Future - Michio Kaku (2011)]] + new [[Thinkers/Michio Kaku]] + 3 concepts ([[Concepts/Kardashev Scale (Kaku)]], [[Concepts/Future of AI - Rise of the Machines (Kaku)]], [[Concepts/Mind over Matter (Kaku)]]); targeted cross-updates to Kelly (12 forces/Cognifying/Becoming as 2100 realizations), Ford (automation complement), Bostrom (AI risks/Ch2), Kurzweil (acceleration with slowing noted), Mitchell (limits exact match), Wiener (cybernetics roots), Hofstadter/Dennett/Turing (brain architecture). Physics basis for AI & CogSci leg + History of Ideas (dynamic Type I–III becoming vs. static Chain). See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Rise of the Robots* (Martin Ford, 2015)** — data-driven argument that accelerating IT, machine learning, robotics, and AI mark a structural break ("Is This Time Different?") from prior automation waves. "Predictable" jobs (learnable from records + big data) across blue- and white-collar sectors (warehouse robots, radiologists, journalists, programmers, office work) are at risk; new industries born labor-saving (Google-scale with tiny headcount). Post-1973 data shows productivity-wage decoupling, jobless recoveries, falling labor share, inequality, and consumer demand erosion ("Machines Do Not Consume"). Past solutions (education, retraining) insufficient. Requires new economic paradigm with guaranteed basic income as central response (sustains demand, stabilizer, may pay for itself). Engages Singularity/super-intelligence (Ch9 with Kurzweil, Hawking et al., skeptics). +1 source [[Sources/Rise of the Robots - Martin Ford (2015)]] + new [[Thinkers/Martin Ford]] + 3 concepts ([[Concepts/Technological Unemployment (Ford)]], [[Concepts/Basic Income Guarantee (Ford)]], [[Concepts/Is This Time Different? (Ford)]]); targeted cross-updates to Kelly (cognifying/becoming as the mechanism), Wiener (Second IR update), Bostrom (Ch9 risks), Kurzweil (Ch9 engagement), Mitchell (capabilities enabling displacement vs. limits). Economic/societal stakes for the AI & CogSci leg. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *What Technology Wants* (Kevin Kelly, 2010)** — the foundational technium thesis: technology as the "seventh kingdom" of life, a global self-organizing evolutionary system (hardware + culture, institutions, intangibles, generative invention) with inherent "wants"/propensities (to self-organize into hierarchies, to perpetuate/expand itself, to increase choices, to increase complexity/exotropy/order and "mind" against entropy, to converge on recurring forms/solutions). Cosmic arc of exotropy (increasing order/mind) from Big Bang → life → technium; humans and technium co-evolve (technology amplifies rather than opposes or replaces deep human nature). Choices within the wants (Amish hackers as selective/convivial model; critique of total rejection). Infinite game (sustain/expand mind, choice, becoming). This is the deeper framework for the 12 forces in *The Inevitable* (2016). +1 source [[Sources/What Technology Wants - Kevin Kelly (2010)]] + 3 concepts ([[Concepts/Technium (Kelly)]], [[Concepts/Exotropy (Kelly)]], [[Concepts/Convergence (Kelly)]]); targeted updates to [[Thinkers/Kevin Kelly]] (now 2 sources, expanded technium section), [[Sources/The Inevitable - Kevin Kelly (2016)]], prior Kelly concepts, Lovejoy/Chain (technium/exotropy/becoming as dynamic evolutionary successor displacing static hierarchy), Kurzweil (wants/trajectories vs acceleration), Bostrom (wants vs orthogonality/instrumental convergence), Mitchell (co-evolution/amplification vs limits/hype). Deep evolutionary context for AI & CogSci leg and History of Ideas (becoming/exotropy overthrows pre-modern static "diagrams"). See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *The Inevitable* (Kevin Kelly, 2016)** — map of 12 overlapping, codependent technological forces (Becoming, Cognifying, Flowing, Screening, Accessing, Sharing, Filtering, Remixing, Interacting, Tracking, Questioning, Beginning) that are "inevitable" in the sense of strong physics/math biases in digital tech (bits, networks, AI) that will shape the next 30 years. Not predictions of specifics but trajectories rooted in the nature of technology; "embrace with eyes wide open" to manage particulars and capture benefits. Future is protopia (incremental improvement amid new problems) rather than utopia/dystopia. We are at the "Beginning" of wiring up the planetary holos (humans + machines as global mind). New source-summary [[Sources/The Inevitable - Kevin Kelly (2016)]] + new [[Thinkers/Kevin Kelly]] + concepts ([[Concepts/The Inevitable (Kelly)]], [[Concepts/Becoming (Kelly)]], [[Concepts/Cognifying (Kelly)]], [[Concepts/Questioning (Kelly)]] + others) + targeted cross-updates to Kurzweil (shared acceleration), Bostrom (risks within the inevitable), Mitchell (limits/hype), Wiener (control), Hofstadter/Pearl (mechanisms), recent AI sources (lived forces in 2026), and Lovejoy/Chain (becoming displaces static diagrams). Optimistic/process map for the AI & CogSci leg. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *The Origins of Modern Science 1300-1800* (Herbert Butterfield, 1949)** — the influential framing of the "Scientific Revolution" (especially 16th–17th c., roots to 14th) as the decisive event in Western history since the rise of Christianity: it "overturned the authority in science not only of the middle ages but of the ancient world", changed "the character of men’s habitual mental operations", transformed the "diagram of the physical universe and the very texture of human life itself", and marks the real origin of the modern world and modern mentality (rendering conventional periodisation an anachronism). Not mere accumulation or great-man biography, but study of pivotal changes in mentality, the older systems overthrown, "misfires," "intellectual hurdles," and "blind alleys." By late 17th c. science begins to "take control" and direct other factors; civilisational leadership shifts northward from the Mediterranean. New source-summary [[Sources/The Origins of Modern Science 1300-1800 - Herbert Butterfield (1949)]] + new [[Thinkers/Herbert Butterfield]] + concept [[Concepts/Scientific Revolution (Butterfield)]] + targeted cross-updates to the Philosophy of Science cluster (especially [[Sources/The Structure of Scientific Revolutions - Kuhn (Chicago, 4th ed.)]], [[Concepts/Scientific Revolutions and Paradigm Shifts (Kuhn)]], and related Kuhn/Popper/Hacking pages — Butterfield's narrative as key background that Kuhn revises with normal science, paradigm shifts as conversions, and incommensurability). Historiographical root for the Phil of Science leg and bridge from History of Ideas (overthrow of pre-modern "diagrams" such as the Great Chain). See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *The Great Chain of Being* (Arthur O. Lovejoy, 1936)** — the definitive history of the "Great Chain of Being" (*scala naturae*) as the dominant presupposition of Western cosmology, theology, science, and reflective poetry for ~2000 years. Isolates the fusion of three unit-ideas — **plenitude** (all possible kinds must exist; generative "good" overflows without envy), **continuity** (no gaps; nature makes no leaps; infinitesimal gradations), **gradation** (single hierarchical *scala* from bare existents to *ens perfectissimum*, each link differing by the least possible degree) — from Plato (*Timaeus*) + Aristotle through Neoplatonism (emanation), medieval theology (Augustine, Pseudo-Dionysius, Aquinas), Leibniz/Spinoza, 18c optimism (Pope), to temporalizing (18c biology, Schelling: God "coming to be") and the "instructive negative outcome": the hypothesis of complete rational intelligibility of the cosmos (sufficient reason for every detail and the world's exact fullness, no arbitrariness) is unbelievable; a temporal world cannot be deduced from eternal necessary truths; plenitude + continuity generate infinities and contradictions (qualitative continuum is incoherent); complete rationality excluding arbitrariness "becomes itself a kind of irrationality"; world is contingent ("Will is prior to Intellect"). Yet the idea had "curiously happy consequences." New source-summary [[Sources/The Great Chain of Being - Arthur O. Lovejoy (1936)]] + new [[Thinkers/Arthur O. Lovejoy]] + 4 concepts ([[Concepts/Great Chain of Being (Lovejoy)]], [[Concepts/Principle of Plenitude (Lovejoy)]], [[Concepts/Principle of Continuity (Lovejoy)]], [[Concepts/Principle of Gradation (Lovejoy)]]) + targeted cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Plato]], [[Thinkers/Aristotle]], [[Thinkers/Plotinus]], [[Thinkers/Thomas Aquinas]], [[Thinkers/Augustine]], [[Concepts/Emanation (Proodos)]], [[Concepts/The One (To Hen)]], and [[Concepts/Strange Loops and Tangled Hierarchies (Hofstadter)]] (static unilinear Chain vs. tangled/emergent). Initiates **History of Ideas** leg; root for presuppositions of classical/medieval cluster and Phil of Science reactions against the static rational cosmos. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Gödel’s Proof* (Ernest Nagel & James R. Newman, 1958; rev. 2001 w/ foreword by Douglas Hofstadter)** — canonical accessible exposition of Gödel's 1931 incompleteness theorems. Gödel numbering arithmetizes syntax (prime-factor encoding of formulas/proofs); meta-statements about provability become arithmetic inside the system. Self-referential G ("G is not provable") is true but unprovable if consistent → incompleteness (and essential incompleteness). Consistency unprovable within the system (2nd thm). Nagel/Newman: limits of axiomatic method and calculating machines. Hofstadter foreword: personal story (teen discovery, friendship with Nagel) + dissent (fixed directives can still yield flexible, analogy-based engines). New source-summary [[Sources/Gödel’s Proof - Ernest Nagel and James R. Newman (1958)]] + new [[Thinkers/Kurt Gödel]] + [[Thinkers/Ernest Nagel]] + [[Thinkers/James R. Newman]] + cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Douglas Hofstadter]] (parent text of GEB + machine disagreement), [[Thinkers/Alan Turing]] (clarifies the mathematical objection), and Hofstadter concepts ([[Concepts/Gödelian Incompleteness and Self-Reference (Hofstadter)]], [[Concepts/Formal Systems and Isomorphism (Hofstadter)]]). Technical foundation for the AI & CogSci self-reference cluster. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *The Book of Why* (Judea Pearl & Dana Mackenzie, 2018)** — the Causal Revolution: formal language for cause and effect (the do-operator, causal diagrams/DAGs, do-calculus, structural causal models). Three-level Ladder of Causation: Association/Seeing (P(Y|X), rung 1 — all of statistics and deep learning), Intervention/Doing (P(Y|do(X)), rung 2 — requires causal diagram), Counterfactuals/Imagining (rung 3 — requires SCMs). Core tools: back-door criterion (deconfounding by blocking noncausal paths), front-door adjustment (identifying causal effects via mediator when confounders unmeasured), do-calculus (3 rules, Shpitser completeness 2006), instrumental variables (Snow cholera, Mendelian randomization). Counterfactuals: abduction→action→prediction; probability of necessity (but-for causation); climate attribution. Mediation: CDE/NDE/NIE, Mediation Formula, scurvy/vitamin C (wrong mediator), Barbara Burks (1926, collider-bias pioneer), Berkeley admissions paradox. Simpson's paradox resolved by causal structure, not statistics. Historical narrative: Galton/Pearson purged causation; Sewall Wright's path diagrams suppressed; Causal Revolution recovers it. Pearl's mini-Turing test: causal reasoning as the criterion for human-level AI; deep learning stuck at rung 1. New source-summary [[Sources/The Book of Why - Judea Pearl (2018)]] + new entity [[Thinkers/Judea Pearl]] + 6 concepts ([[Concepts/Ladder of Causation (Pearl)]], [[Concepts/Do-Operator and Do-Calculus (Pearl)]], [[Concepts/Causal Diagrams and DAGs (Pearl)]], [[Concepts/Confounding and Back-Door Criterion (Pearl)]], [[Concepts/Counterfactuals and Structural Causal Models (Pearl)]], [[Concepts/Mediation and Direct-Indirect Effects (Pearl)]]) + 1 new contradiction ([[Contradictions/Pearl vs. Statistics Orthodoxy - Causation Purged and Recovered]]). Cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Alan Turing]] (mini-Turing test challenge to imitation game), [[Thinkers/Daniel Dennett]] (Freedom Evolves cited; compatibilism convergence), [[Thinkers/Hume]] (but-for causation as rung-3 ancestor). Stats updated. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Consciousness Explained* (Daniel C. Dennett, 1991)** — the vault's primary naturalized functionalist philosophy of mind: Multiple Drafts Model (parallel distributed editorial processes replacing the Cartesian Theater), heterophenomenology (neutral third-person method for phenomenology), qualia deflation (Mary the color scientist, inverted qualia, epiphenomenal qualia — all dissolved), self as Center of Narrative Gravity, Joycean virtual machine (culturally installed serial VM on parallel brain hardware), memes + Baldwin Effect (cultural evolution of consciousness). New source-summary [[Sources/Consciousness Explained - Daniel C. Dennett (1991)]] + new entity [[Thinkers/Daniel Dennett]] + 6 concepts ([[Concepts/Multiple Drafts Model (Dennett)]], [[Concepts/Cartesian Theater (Dennett)]], [[Concepts/Heterophenomenology (Dennett)]], [[Concepts/Qualia Disqualified (Dennett)]], [[Concepts/Center of Narrative Gravity (Dennett)]], [[Concepts/Joycean Machine and Memes (Dennett)]]) + 1 new contradiction ([[Contradictions/Dennett vs Nagel - Multiple Drafts vs Subjective Experience]]). Cross-updates to [[Thinkers/John Searle]] (Systems Reply as canonical rejoinder to Chinese Room), [[Thinkers/Douglas Hofstadter]] (Dennett as ally; different mechanism; co-editor of *The Mind's I*), [[Thinkers/Alan Turing]] (Dennett as later defender of Turing-style functionalism), [[Contradictions/Hofstadter vs Searle - Strange Loops vs Chinese Room]] (Dennett as third voice). Stats updated. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *The Human Use of Human Beings* (Norbert Wiener, 1950)** — the founding text of cybernetics as social philosophy and the earliest clear statement of the AI alignment problem. New source-summary [[Sources/The Human Use of Human Beings - Norbert Wiener (1950)]] + new entity [[Thinkers/Norbert Wiener]] + 6 concepts ([[Concepts/Cybernetics (Wiener)]], [[Concepts/Feedback and Control (Wiener)]], [[Concepts/Entropy and Information (Wiener)]], [[Concepts/Learning vs Rigidity (Wiener)]], [[Concepts/Alignment Problem - Sorcerer's Apprentice (Wiener)]], [[Concepts/Second Industrial Revolution (Wiener)]]) + 1 new contradiction ([[Contradictions/Wiener vs Shannon - Information and Meaning]]). Core: society understandable only through messages; organism = pattern not substance ("We are but whirlpools in a river of ever-flowing water"); humans as learning beings vs. rigid ants; alignment problem in 1950 (Monkey's Paw / Bottled Djinnee; "the machine will in no way be obliged to make such decisions as we should have made"); second IR = machines displacing judgment = precise economic equivalent of slave labor; information is not a commodity. Cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Claude Shannon]] (shared entropy math; opposed on semantics — see contradiction) and [[Thinkers/Alan Turing]] (co-1950 AI founding; Wiener adds social/alignment analysis Turing omits). Stats updated. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Gödel, Escher, Bach* (Douglas Hofstadter, 1979)** — the vault's canonical pro-computationalist account of consciousness and AI; the major alternative to Searle's Chinese Room in the AI & Cognitive Science leg. New source-summary [[Sources/Gödel, Escher, Bach - Douglas Hofstadter (1979)]] + new entity [[Thinkers/Douglas Hofstadter]] + 5 concepts ([[Concepts/Strange Loops and Tangled Hierarchies (Hofstadter)]], [[Concepts/Formal Systems and Isomorphism (Hofstadter)]], [[Concepts/Gödelian Incompleteness and Self-Reference (Hofstadter)]], [[Concepts/Emergence and Consciousness (Hofstadter)]], [[Concepts/Reductionism vs Holism (Hofstadter)]]) + 1 new contradiction ([[Contradictions/Hofstadter vs Searle - Strange Loops vs Chinese Room]]). Core: strange loops (self-referential feedback across hierarchy levels) as the structural mechanism of consciousness; meaning arises through isomorphism (structure-preserving mappings); Gödel incompleteness as a mirror of mind; tangled hierarchy as the dissolution of reductionism vs. holism; strong AI possible in principle. Cross-references to Turing (halting problem as computational parallel), Searle (isomorphism vs. Chinese Room), Shannon (semantics bracketed vs. emergent), Descartes (dualism dissolved by emergence). Stats updated. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *A Mathematical Theory of Communication* (C. E. Shannon, 1948)** — the canonical foundation of information theory and the communication counterpart to the vault's Turing (1950) source. New source-summary [[Sources/A Mathematical Theory of Communication - Claude Shannon (1948)]] + entity [[Thinkers/Claude Shannon]] + 6 concepts ([[Concepts/Entropy (Shannon)]], [[Concepts/Channel Capacity (Shannon)]], [[Concepts/Redundancy (Shannon)]], [[Concepts/Equivocation (Shannon)]], [[Concepts/Noiseless Channel Coding Theorem]], [[Concepts/Noisy Channel Coding Theorem]]). Core: information as uncertainty (H = −∑ p_i log p_i in bits); general system (source → transmitter → noisy channel → receiver → destination); semantics bracketed for engineering; noiseless theorem (transmit at C/H); noisy theorem (reliable comm at rates ≤ C with coding; equivocation ≥ H−C above C); English ~50% redundancy from statistical structure (approximations, deletion experiments). Directly grounds AI (prediction = entropy minimization; context/scaffolds/coding for reliable transmission over stochastic "channels"). Cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Alan Turing]] (info + computation), modern AI sources (Grok/Claude/Gemini tutorials, Daily Tips), [[Thinkers/Garry Kasparov]], and epistemology of uncertainty. Stats updated. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Computing Machinery and Intelligence* (A. M. Turing, 1950)** — the canonical origin point for the operational study of machine intelligence and the bridge into the AI & Cognitive Science leg (full prior banner text preserved in git history or earlier index versions).

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Representing and Intervening* (Ian Hacking, 1983)** — the experimental turn that complements the vault's representation- and rationality-focused philosophy-of-science cluster (Popper–Kuhn–Feyerabend–Quine–Ayer). New source-summary + entity + 5 concepts (entity realism, "if you can spray them then they are real", creation of phenomena, experiment has a life of its own, observation as a skill). Extended [[Contradictions/Popper vs Kuhn - Falsification vs Paradigms]]. Cross-updates to Kuhn source/thinker, Feyerabend, Popper, Incommensurability. Core: two realisms (entities vs. theories); experiment grounds realism about entities via intervention. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Language, Truth and Logic* (A.J. Ayer, 1936)** — the founding English-language manifesto of logical positivism; the position Popper, Kuhn, and Quine are all explicitly reacting against. New source [[Sources/Language, Truth and Logic - A.J. Ayer (1936)]] + new entity [[Thinkers/A.J. Ayer]] + 4 new concepts ([[Concepts/Verification Principle (Ayer)]], [[Concepts/Metaphysics as Meaningless (Ayer)]], [[Concepts/Analytic Propositions as Tautologies (Ayer)]], [[Concepts/Emotive Theory of Ethics (Ayer)]]). Cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Hume]] (Ayer as logical-positivist heir), [[Concepts/Hume's Fork (Relations of Ideas vs Matters of Fact)]] (verification principle as sharpening), and [[Concepts/Reductionism and Verification Theory of Meaning (Quine)]] (Ayer as primary target). Stats: sources 48, thinkers 28, concepts 147, total ~234. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Two Dogmas of Empiricism* (W.V.O. Quine, 1951/1961)** — the foundational critique of logical empiricism that opens the post-positivist turn. New source-summary [[Sources/Two Dogmas of Empiricism - W.V.O. Quine (1951)]] + new entity [[Thinkers/W.V.O. Quine]] + 4 new concepts ([[Concepts/Analytic-Synthetic Distinction (Quine)]], [[Concepts/Reductionism and Verification Theory of Meaning (Quine)]], [[Concepts/Confirmation Holism (Duhem-Quine Thesis)]], [[Concepts/Naturalized Epistemology and the Web of Belief (Quine)]]). Cross-updates to [[Concepts/Hume's Fork (Relations of Ideas vs Matters of Fact)]] (Quine attacks the fork's sharpness), [[Concepts/Problem of Induction (Hume)]], [[Concepts/Problem of Demarcation (Kant and Popper)]] (deepens the Carnap/verificationism critique), [[Concepts/Falsifiability as Criterion of Demarcation (Popper)]], and the Popper/Kuhn/Russell thinker pages (holism vs. localized testing; meaning change and underdetermination affinities with Kuhn). Quine dismantles the analytic/synthetic distinction and reductionism, yields confirmation holism (web of belief; unit of significance = whole of science), treats physical objects etc. as pragmatic cultural posits on a par with gods, and blurs metaphysics/science while shifting toward thorough pragmatism and naturalized epistemology. Primary source for the leg's critique of the dogmas Popper and Kuhn also react against. Stats updated. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *The Structure of Scientific Revolutions* (Thomas Kuhn, 1962 / Chicago 4th ed.)** — the major counterweight to Popper in the Philosophy of Science leg. New source-summary [[Sources/The Structure of Scientific Revolutions - Kuhn (Chicago, 4th ed.)]] + new entity [[Thinkers/Thomas Kuhn]] + 5 new concepts ([[Concepts/Paradigm (Kuhn)]], [[Concepts/Normal Science and Puzzle-Solving (Kuhn)]], [[Concepts/Anomaly and Crisis (Kuhn)]], [[Concepts/Scientific Revolutions and Paradigm Shifts (Kuhn)]], [[Concepts/Incommensurability (Kuhn)]]) + new dedicated [[Contradictions/Popper vs Kuhn - Falsification vs Paradigms]]. Cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Karl Popper]] and the Popper cluster ([[Concepts/Falsifiability as Criterion of Demarcation (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Corroboration (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Verisimilitude (Truthlikeness) (Popper)]]) + both Popper source pages (Kuhn challenge now primary-sourced). Kuhn replaces cumulative-verification and Popperian permanent-falsification with normal science (puzzle-solving under a paradigm) → anomaly → crisis → revolution (gestalt-switch/conversion), incommensurable paradigms, and progress as "evolution *from*, not *toward*" truth. The vault's first history/sociology-of-science source. Stats updated. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *Conjectures and Refutations* (Karl Popper, 1963 / Routledge Classics 2002)** — the companion to *LSD*. New source-summary [[Sources/Conjectures and Refutations - Karl Popper]] + 4 new concepts ([[Concepts/No Authoritative Sources of Knowledge (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Three Views Concerning Human Knowledge (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Verisimilitude (Truthlikeness) (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Critique of Historicism and Dialectic (Popper)]]) + major updates to the 3 existing Popper concepts ([[Concepts/Falsifiability as Criterion of Demarcation (Popper)]] with the Marx/Freud/Adler-vs-Einstein illustration + seven conclusions; [[Concepts/Problem of Demarcation (Kant and Popper)]] with the Carnap critique + "false yet irrefutable"; [[Concepts/Corroboration (Popper)]] with verisimilitude + the three requirements for growth) + expansion of [[Thinkers/Karl Popper]] (now 2 primary sources) + cross-links to [[Concepts/Correspondence Theory of Truth (Russell)]] (ally) and [[Concepts/Pragmatic Theory of Truth (James)]] (opponent). Adds Popper's scientific realism, the sources-of-knowledge critique, verisimilitude, the Presocratic critical tradition, and — for the first time in primary form — Popper's **political philosophy** (anti-historicism, prophecy vs. prediction, open society, piecemeal engineering). **Also fixed prior index drift**: the LSD ingest never added catalog lines — Popper Sources/Thinker/Concepts now entered below; stats corrected. See [[log]] for full entry.

Prior: 2026-06-04 — **Ingest of *The Logic of Scientific Discovery* (Karl Popper, Routledge Classics)**. New source-summary + new [[Thinkers/Karl Popper]] + 4 new core concepts ([[Concepts/Falsifiability as Criterion of Demarcation (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Corroboration (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Basic Statements and the Empirical Basis (Popper)]], [[Concepts/Problem of Demarcation (Kant and Popper)]]). Major cross-updates to [[Concepts/Problem of Induction (Hume)]], [[Thinkers/Hume]], [[Thinkers/Bertrand Russell]], [[Thinkers/Kant]], [[Thinkers/Epicurus]], and related Hume/Enquiry source page. Popper supplies the primary 20th-c. philosophy-of-science treatment and the methodological response to Hume's induction problem (falsifiability + deductive testing + corroboration), advancing the vault toward the anticipated Philosophy of Science / Epistemology leg. See [[log]] for full entry. Index/Overview counts and entries updated.

Prior: 2026-06-04 (**Sun Tzu, *The Art of War*** (Lionel Giles trans., 1910) — the vault's **first work of strategy/statecraft and first non-Western classic**. Thirteen aphoristic chapters on war, competition, and intelligence: [[Concepts/Calculation and the Five Factors (Sun Tzu)|calculation of the five factors]] (Moral Law/*tao*, Heaven, Earth, Commander, Method) before battle ("many calculations lead to victory"); [[Concepts/Winning Without Fighting (Sun Tzu)|"supreme excellence consists in breaking the enemy's resistance without fighting"]] (take whole and intact; attack the enemy's *plans*; siege is worst); [[Concepts/Know the Enemy and Know Yourself (Sun Tzu)|"if you know the enemy and know yourself, you need not fear a hundred battles"]]; [[Concepts/All Warfare Is Based on Deception (Sun Tzu)|"all warfare is based on deception"]] (seem far when near; control the enemy's perception); [[Concepts/Shih - Momentum, Formlessness, and Striking Weakness (Sun Tzu)|direct/indirect, momentum (*shih*), and water-like formlessness]] ("avoid what is strong and strike at what is weak"; "no constant tactics"; wind/forest/fire/mountain); and [[Concepts/Foreknowledge and the Use of Spies (Sun Tzu)|foreknowledge from human intelligence]] (the five classes of spies; "cannot be obtained inductively from experience… only from other men"). New entity ([[Thinkers/Sun Tzu]]) + 6 concepts; cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Thucydides]] / [[Concepts/Power, Empire, and Revolt (Thucydides)]] (the Western descriptive-realist counterpart) and [[Thinkers/Garry Kasparov]] (chess as Sun Tzu's laboratory). Strong transfer to career/competition, negotiation, AI/agentic planning, and game theory; ethics/just-war tension with Augustine/Kant flagged. See [[log]] for details.

Prior: **Bertrand Russell, *The Problems of Philosophy*** (1912) — Russell's classic short *primary* epistemology (companion to his 1945 survey, same [[Thinkers/Bertrand Russell]] entity). Constructive theory of knowledge: [[Concepts/Sense-Data vs. Physical Objects (Russell)|sense-data vs. physical objects]] (appearance/reality; the table; structural realism — we know matter's *relations* not its intrinsic nature; idealism refuted via the act/object distinction); [[Concepts/Knowledge by Acquaintance and Knowledge by Description (Russell)|knowledge by acquaintance vs. by description]] ("every proposition we can understand must be composed wholly of constituents with which we are acquainted"; description lets us pass beyond private experience); the restatement of [[Concepts/Problem of Induction (Hume)|Hume's induction problem]] (the **chicken**; the inductive principle as a priori); a qualified realism about **universals**; the [[Concepts/Correspondence Theory of Truth (Russell)|correspondence theory of truth]] ("minds do not *create* truth… what makes a belief true is a *fact*"; coherence rejected as *definition*, kept as *test*); and [[Concepts/The Value of Philosophy (Russell)|the value of philosophy]] ("studied not for definite answers but for the questions"; enlargement of the Self; the free intellect; explicit rejection of "truth is man-made"). 4 new concepts; cross-updates to the Russell entity (now 2 sources), [[Concepts/Problem of Induction (Hume)]], [[Concepts/Method of Hyperbolic Doubt (Descartes)]], and [[Concepts/Pragmatic Theory of Truth (James)]] (Russell's correspondence theory as the positive realist counter — his anti-pragmatism was lifelong). See [[log]] for details.

Prior: **Bertrand Russell, *A History of Western Philosophy*** (1945) — Russell's encyclopedic, opinionated survey of the whole Western tradition (Thales → logical analysis), ingested as a *meta-layer* over nearly every thinker the vault holds. Distinctive value: (1) the **social-circumstances method** (philosophy as "both an effect and a cause of the character of the various communities in which different systems flourished"); (2) **philosophy as the "No Man's Land" between theology and science** (its gift: "to live without certainty, yet without being paralyzed"); (3) the master political dialectic **social cohesion vs. individual liberty** (disciplinarians vs. libertarians; liberalism as escape from the oscillation); (4) Russell's own **philosophy of logical analysis** (piecemeal, scientific, "successive approximations — improvement not rejection") + the **"cosmic impiety"** warning; and (5) his sharp **verdicts** on vault thinkers — Spinoza "the noblest and most lovable," Aquinas "special pleading," Plato "hardly ever intellectually honest," Hume's empiricism "made self-consistent… incredible," Nietzsche "I dislike Nietzsche…," and the realist critique of the just-added pragmatic theory of truth. New source + entity (Bertrand Russell) + 3 concepts (No Man's Land; Social Cohesion vs. Individual Liberty; Philosophy of Logical Analysis); cross-updates to Spinoza, Aquinas, Plato, Nietzsche, Hume, William James, and [[Concepts/Pragmatic Theory of Truth (James)]]. The vault's first 20th-century analytic philosopher and a meta-survey verdict-layer over the tradition. See [[log]] for details.

Prior: **William James, *Pragmatism: A New Name for Some Old Ways of Thinking*** (1907, eight Lowell/Columbia lectures) — pragmatism as both a *method* (settle disputes by tracing practical consequences; "no difference that makes no difference"; theories as instruments) and a *genetic theory of truth* ("truth *happens* to an idea… made true by verification"; "the true is the expedient"; truth lives "on a credit system"); the tender-minded vs. tough-minded temperaments; the one and the many (oneness/manyness coordinate → pluralism; Absolute vs. Ultimate); common sense as inherited *denkmittel*; Schiller's humanism ("the world is malleable… man engenders truths upon it"; "we carve the statue"); meliorism (salvation as a grounded *possibility* between optimism and pessimism) + pluralistic theism (God as *primus inter pares*). New source + new entity (William James) + 6 concepts (Pragmatic Method, Pragmatic Theory of Truth, Tender-Minded and Tough-Minded, The One and the Many, Meliorism, Humanism-Reality as Malleable); cross-updates to [[Thinkers/Hume]] (pragmatism as the constructive heir of empiricism) and [[Concepts/Perspectivism (Nietzsche)]] (truth/reality as man-made). The vault's first American pragmatist and its third path on truth (alongside classical adequation and rationalist correspondence). See [[log]] for details. *(Date note: continues the wiki's internal chronology, which runs ahead of the 2026-06-04 system clock.)*

Prior: full *On the Genealogy of Morals* (1887) — Preface + three essays (master/slave morality, ressentiment as creative engine of the slave revolt, "good and evil" vs. "good and bad," Rome vs. Judaea, sovereign individual + proud conscience as memory of the will, bad conscience as internalization/illness/pregnancy, "ought" from "owe," punishment's many meanings, ascetic ideal + "horror vacui: he needs a goal and will sooner will nothingness than not will," genealogy as method); prior: BGE chunk 1 + full Kant + cross-polish + full Locke + lint). New source + 5 new concepts (Ressentiment, Master Morality and Slave Morality, Bad Conscience, Ascetic Ideal, Sovereign Individual); updates to Nietzsche entity (expanded with full Genealogy positions and vault models), BGE source (noted as explicit continuation), index/Overview/log, and cross-cluster pages (Kant/Hume/Spinoza/Plato etc. with "Vs. Genealogy" notes). The analytic heart of the revaluation now primary-sourced; genealogical suspicion and sovereign promising as major new tools for the entire ethics cluster and vault method. Updated stats, Sources/Entities/Concepts sections. No contradictions. See [[log]] for details.

## Categories

### Sources

Source summaries and raw material references. One page per major ingested document is ideal.

- [[Sources/Phenomenology of Perception - Maurice Merleau-Ponty (1945)]] — Maurice Merleau-Ponty, *Phénoménologie de la perception* (Gallimard, 1945); Donald A. Landes trans. (Routledge 2012). First MP primary. Incomplete reduction; one's own body; *I can* / intentional arc; speech accomplishes thought; coexistence not analogy; tacit cogito; situated freedom. EPUB filename said Smith. Created 1 thinker + 7 concepts + 1 contradiction. | source-summary | phenomenology, philosophy-of-mind, epistemology, ontology
- [[Sources/Naming and Necessity - Saul Kripke (1980)]] — Saul A. Kripke, three Princeton lectures (20/22/29 Jan 1970); Harvard 1980 book with preface. Names are rigid designators; a priori ≠ necessary; Frege–Russell/cluster theory fails; baptism + chain of communication is a *picture*; natural-kind identifications and Hesperus/Phosphorus are necessary a posteriori; pain is not a Hesperus case. 2-D is Chalmers's reception. WRPL is a different book. | source-summary | philosophy-of-language, metaphysics, epistemology, philosophy-of-mind
- [[Sources/Putnam on BIVs and Radical Skepticism - Pritchard and Ranalli (2016)]] — Duncan Pritchard and Chris Ranalli, ch. 6 of Goldberg (ed.), *The Brain in a Vat* (CUP 2016), 75–89. Reconstructs Putnam's semantic BIV argument (not a knowledge claim) and the Brueckner / Wright / Stroud / Nagel attacks. Queue file was the volume ISBN; disk file is this chapter only. Putnam 1981 still outstanding. | source-summary | epistemology, philosophy-of-language, metaphysics
- [[Sources/The Crooked Timber of Humanity - Isaiah Berlin (2013)]] — Isaiah Berlin, *The Crooked Timber of Humanity: Chapters in the History of Ideas*, 2nd ed., ed. Henry Hardy (Princeton, 2013; first ed. 1990). First Berlin primary. Value pluralism; pluralism ≠ relativism; utopia as conceptually incoherent; Counter-Enlightenment (Vico, Herder, Maistre); Romantic will; bent-twig nationalism. Not “Two Concepts of Liberty.” | source-summary | history-of-ideas, political-philosophy, ethics
- [[Sources/Isaiah Berlin and the Politics of Freedom - Baum and Nichols (2013)]] — Bruce Baum and Robert Nichols (eds.), *Isaiah Berlin and the Politics of Freedom: “Two Concepts of Liberty” 50 Years Later* (Routledge, 2013). Critical commemorative collection; reception of the 1958 lecture, not the lecture. Negative/positive liberty, value pluralism, liberty vs its conditions; Tully vs Crowder. Created 1 thinker + 3 concepts + 1 contradiction. *Crooked Timber* now a separate primary. | source-summary | political-philosophy, history-of-ideas
- [[Sources/Anarchy, State, and Utopia - Robert Nozick (1974)]] — Robert Nozick (Basic Books, 1974). Individuals have rights so strong they raise the question of what, if anything, the state may do. Invisible-hand justification of the night-watchman state; entitlement theory vs Rawls; experience machine; utopia as a filter of voluntary communities. First libertarian primary. | source-summary | political-philosophy, ethics, political-economy
- [[Sources/The Analects - Confucius (Chin trans., 2014)]] — *Lunyu*, Annping Chin trans. and commentary (Penguin 2014). EPUB filename said 1979; file is Chin, not Lau. Fragmentary compiled sayings plus Qing-evidential apparatus (Liu Baonan). Humaneness, rites, gentleman, filiality, rectifying names, *shu*. First Confucian primary. Created 1 thinker + 1 school + 6 concepts + 1 contradiction. Mencius/Xunzi still outstanding. | source-summary | ethics, political-philosophy, philosophy-of-language
- [[Sources/Daodejing - Laozi (Ziporyn trans., 2023)]] — Laozi, *Daodejing* (received 81 chapters; Brook Ziporyn trans., Liveright 2023). Composite commonplace book, not a single-authored treatise. Ironic *dao* as "course," *de* as unlearned virtuosity, nondoing, unhewn A/B reversal, water/low place. The vault's first Chinese philosophical primary. Confucian counterpart now in. Created 1 thinker + 1 school + 5 concepts. | source-summary | metaphysics, ethics, political-philosophy, philosophy-of-language
- [[Sources/Descartes' Error - Antonio Damasio (1994)]] — Antonio Damasio, *Descartes' Error: Emotion, Reason, and the Human Brain* (1994; 2005 Penguin reprint). Emotion in the loop of reason (somatic-marker hypothesis); feeling as perception of a body landscape; mind exists in and for an organism; Descartes's abyssal split is the named error. Created 1 thinker + 4 concepts. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, neuroscience, affective-science
- [[Sources/Being You - Anil Seth (2021)]] — Anil Seth, *Being You: A New Science of Consciousness* (Faber/Dutton, 2021). Real-problem method + beast-machine inversion: we are conscious selves *because* we are living bodies that predict and control their physiology. Controlled hallucination now primary from both Sussex colleagues. Created 1 thinker + 9 concepts + 1 contradiction. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, neuroscience
- [[Sources/Consciousness and the Brain - Stanislas Dehaene (2014)]] — Stanislas Dehaene, *Consciousness and the Brain* (Viking/Penguin, 2014). Global neuronal workspace as primary: consciousness = conscious access = cortical broadcasting, indexed by late P3/ignition/brain-web signatures. Chalmers's hard problem treated as mislabeled vitalism. Created 1 thinker + 5 concepts + 1 contradiction. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, neuroscience, ai
- [[Sources/Cartesian Meditations - Edmund Husserl]] — Edmund Husserl, *Cartesianische Meditationen* (1929 Sorbonne lectures; Fr. 1931; Ger. Husserliana I 1950; Dorion Cairns trans., Nijhoff 1960). First Husserl primary: neo-Cartesianism that keeps the radical beginning and rejects the doctrine; §41 idealism; pairing as constitution of the Other; monadology of transcendental intersubjectivity. Corrects two Smith glosses. | source-summary | phenomenology, epistemology, ontology, philosophy-of-mind
- [[Sources/Being and Nothingness - Jean-Paul Sartre (Barnes trans.)]] — Jean-Paul Sartre, *L'être et le néant* (1943); Hazel E. Barnes trans. Phenomenological ontology: two regions of being, nothingness from the question, bad faith, the Look, freedom-in-situation, existential psychoanalysis, useless passion. Systematic primary behind the Welten biography and the 1945/46 lecture. | source-summary | ontology, phenomenology, existentialism, ethics
- [[Sources/Fear and Trembling - Kierkegaard (Hannay 1985)]] — Søren Kierkegaard, *Frygt og Bæven* (1843); Hannay trans., Penguin 1985. Dialectical lyric by Johannes *de silentio*. Faith as double movement; teleological suspension; single individual higher than the universal. First Kierkegaard primary. Created 1 thinker + 3 concepts + 1 argument + 2 contradictions. | source-summary | ethics, philosophy-of-religion, existentialism
- [[Sources/Existentialism is a Humanism - Jean-Paul Sartre (1946)]] — Jean-Paul Sartre, *L'existentialisme est un humanisme* (1945/46); Philip Mairet trans. in Kaufmann, *Existentialism from Dostoevsky to Sartre*. Public defence of atheistic existentialism: existence precedes essence, condemned to be free, two humanisms. First Sartre primary; makes the Welten slogan pages canonical. | source-summary | existentialism, ethics, ontology
- [[Sources/The Extended Mind - Andy Clark and David Chalmers (1998)]] — Andy Clark and David Chalmers, *Analysis* 58.1 (January 1998): 7–19. Active externalism: reliably coupled external processes (Tetris rotation, Otto's notebook) are parts of cognition and of standing belief; experiences need not extend. Makes [[Concepts/Extended Mind (Clark and Chalmers)]] canonical. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, epistemology
- [[Sources/The Predictive Mind - Jakob Hohwy (2013)]] — Jakob Hohwy, *The Predictive Mind* (OUP 2013). Systematic **prediction-error minimization**: one reiterated mechanism is all the brain does; the mind is secluded behind a veil of sensory input. Counterpart to Clark's popularization; named split now a contradiction. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, neuroscience
- [[Sources/The Experience Machine - Andy Clark (2023)]] — Andy Clark, *The Experience Machine* (Pantheon, 2023). Popular synthesis of **predictive processing**: the brain as a multilevel prediction machine building experience as "controlled hallucination," unifying perception, action, emotion, psychiatry, the extended mind, and a deflationary take on the hard problem. Created 1 thinker + 8 concepts; updated Dennett. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, neuroscience
- [[Sources/Surfing Uncertainty - Andy Clark (2016)]] — Andy Clark, *Surfing Uncertainty* (OUP 2016). Technical PP statement: proactive predictivores; cognitive package deal; action-oriented engagement machine; productive laziness. Systematic reply to Hohwy (Ch 6). 2023 popularizes this; does not yet deflate the hard problem. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, neuroscience

- [[Sources/The History of Sexuality Volume 1 - Michel Foucault (1976)]] - Michel Foucault, *La Volonté de savoir* (1976; Hurley trans., Pantheon 1978). The vault's second primary Foucault text: repressive hypothesis dismantled; *scientia sexualis* vs *ars erotica*; deployment of sexuality; bio-power (foster life / disallow it). Created 3 concepts; rewrote the Macey sexuality and biopower pages from Hurley. | source-summary | political-philosophy, epistemology, ethics, history
- [[Sources/Discipline and Punish - Michel Foucault (1975)]] - Michel Foucault, *Surveiller et punir* (1975; Sheridan trans., Vintage 1995). The vault's first primary Foucault text: genealogy of the modern soul and a new power to judge. Four rules; micro-physics; "the soul is the prison of the body"; panopticism as diagram; delinquency as the prison's product. Created 3 concepts; rewrote the Macey disciplinary page from Sheridan. Companion: HS1. | source-summary | political-philosophy, epistemology, history, sociology
- [[Sources/Michel Foucault - David Macey]] - David Macey, *Michel Foucault* (Reaktion Books, 2004). Intellectual biography tracing all three phases: archaeology (*Folie et déraison*, *Les Mots et les choses*), genealogy (*Surveiller et punir*, *La Volonté de savoir*, GIP activism), ethics of the self (Collège lectures, Greco-Roman askesis). Created 1 thinker + 10 concept pages. | source-summary | biography, poststructuralism, power, epistemology, ethics
- [[Sources/The Deleuze Reader]] - Gilles Deleuze, ed. Constantin V. Boundas (Columbia UP, 1993). 29 excerpts from Deleuze's major works in 5 parts: rhizome ontology; difference and repetition; desire and schizoanalysis; minor literature, nomad art, and cinema; politics. The vault's foundational Deleuze source. Created 15 pages: 1 source, 1 thinker, 13 concepts. | source-summary | metaphysics, ethics, political-philosophy, aesthetics, poststructuralism
- [[Sources/Sartre - Ruud Welten (2020)]] - Ruud Welten, *Sartre* (*Elementaire Deeltjes* 71, Athenaeum, 2020; Dutch). Compact chronological intellectual biography of Jean-Paul Sartre as one restless, unfinished search: phenomenology (intentionality, néantisation, contingency, emotion), *Being and Nothingness* (pour-soi/en-soi, existence precedes essence, bad faith, freedom, the Look), existential psychoanalysis (Baudelaire/Genet/Flaubert), engaged literature, and the *Critique of Dialectical Reason* (totalisation, series/group, fraternity-terror, violence/colonialism with Fanon). The vault's first Sartre source. New thinker + 10 concepts + 1 contradiction. | source-summary | existentialism, phenomenology, ontology, ethics, political-philosophy
- [[Sources/Early Greek Philosophy - John Burnet (1920)]] - John Burnet, *Early Greek Philosophy* (3rd ed. 1920); the classic scholarly translation and doxography of the Pre-Socratic fragments.
- [[Sources/Symposium - Plato]] - Plato's *Symposium* (Jowett trans.); speeches on Love (Eros) and the metaphysical ascent to the Form of Beauty.

- [[Sources/Timaeus and Critias - Plato (Oxford, 2008)]] - Oxford World's Classics edition (Waterfield trans., Gregory intro/notes): Timaeus (teleological cosmogony, demiurge, receptacle, geometric elements, human as microcosm) + unfinished Critias (sole ancient source of Atlantis myth: concentric island empire, bull rituals, war with ancient Athens, cataclysm). Esoteric "technology of managing reality" via selection not struggle. Sampled; full depth pending. Thematic bridge from Plato (order/chaos, intention) to personal agency/career.
- [[Sources/The Republic - Plato (Cambridge, 2000)]] - Cambridge Texts ed. of Plato's Republic (justice, ideal city, philosopher-kings, Forms, Cave allegory, tripartite soul, regime decay). - [[Sources/CPCU 552 Commercial Property Insurance - Study Materials]] - The Institutes CPCU commercial property curriculum (14 files: core glossary/FC + 7 modules on assessing needs/BPP/BIC, business income/EML/worksheet, tailoring, natural disaster gaps/NFIP, transit, critical loss exposures + 6 workbooks). Direct professional development for agribusiness risk consulting and CPCU credential.
- [[Sources/MasterClass Collection - 2026]] - Large 2026 collection (~55+ PDFs + BBQ notes) of MasterClass lectures across storytelling, culinary craft, business/leadership/entrepreneurship (Pink, Schultz, Blakely, Wintour, Iger, Kasparov chess, Voss negotiation, etc.), creative/performing arts, personal mastery (Walker sleep, Kabat Zinn mindfulness), strategy/decision games. One synthesized hub page with thematic clusters + cross-cutting lessons (lived-pain origins, deliberate practice, human creativity vs machines, routines, values+profits, leverage). Strong ties to career, AI, and philosophy threads.
- [[Sources/The Embodied Mind - Varela Thompson and Rosch (2016)]] — Francisco J. Varela, Evan Thompson, and Eleanor Rosch, *The Embodied Mind* (MIT 1991; revised 2016). Cognition as enaction, not recovery of a pregiven world; natural drift; mindfulness/awareness as method; Madhyamaka groundlessness. 2016 recantations on Husserl, Buddhist modernism, and introspection. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, cognitive-science, phenomenology
- [[Sources/What Computers Still Can't Do - Hubert Dreyfus (1992)]] — Hubert Dreyfus, *What Computers Still Can't Do* (MIT Press, 1992; 1972/1979 treatise). Heideggerian critique of artificial reason: GOFAI as Lakatosian degenerating program; four assumptions (biological, psychological, epistemological, ontological); commonsense as know-how; five-stage skill model; body and being-in-the-world as conditions of intelligence. Collision of the Heidegger cluster with the AI leg. | source-summary | ai, phenomenology, philosophy-of-mind
- [[Sources/Computing Machinery and Intelligence - Alan Turing (1950)]] - A. M. Turing (1950) *Mind* paper: Imitation Game as operational substitute for the vague "Can machines think?"; digital computers as universal discrete-state machines; prediction that ~10^9 storage + programming will let machines play the game so well an average interrogator is correct <70% after 5 minutes by ~2000; systematic rebuttal of nine classic objections (Jefferson consciousness, Lady Lovelace "no pretensions to originate", mathematical/Gödel, etc.); research programme for learning machines (child machines with minimal mechanism + education, evolution analogy, random element). Foundational primary source for the vault's AI & Cognitive Science leg and for [[Thinkers/Alan Turing]] + the Turing concept cluster. Bridge from completed Phil of Science leg.
- [[Sources/Minds, Brains and Science - John Searle (1984)]] - John Searle, 1984 Reith Lectures (book 1984/1985): biological naturalism (minds caused by and realized in brains); decisive refutation of strong AI via the Chinese Room (syntax is not sufficient for semantics/understanding). Direct critique of the computational theory of mind. See [[Thinkers/John Searle]] + 5 concepts (Chinese Room Argument, Strong AI, Syntax and Semantics, Biological Naturalism, Intentionality). Major counterweight to Turing computationalism and literal information-processing models in the AI & Cognitive Science leg.
- [[Sources/Gödel, Escher, Bach - Douglas Hofstadter (1979)]] - Douglas Hofstadter (1979/Pulitzer 1980): strange loops (self-referential feedback across hierarchy levels) as the structural mechanism of consciousness and the "I"; meaning arises through isomorphism (structure-preserving mappings between formal system and external domain); Gödel incompleteness as mirror of mind (self-reference irrepressible); tangled hierarchy dissolves reductionism/holism; strong AI possible in principle. Direct computationalist counterweight to Searle's Chinese Room. See [[Thinkers/Douglas Hofstadter]] + 5 concepts + [[Contradictions/Hofstadter vs Searle - Strange Loops vs Chinese Room]].
- [[Sources/Gödel’s Proof - Ernest Nagel and James R. Newman (1958)]] - Ernest Nagel & James R. Newman (1958; rev. ed. 2001 w/ new foreword by Douglas Hofstadter): the canonical accessible yet rigorous exposition of Gödel's 1931 incompleteness theorems. Gödel numbering (prime-factor encoding) arithmetizes metamathematics so PM can talk about its own proofs; constructs self-referential G ("G is not provable") that is true but unprovable if consistent; proves essential incompleteness and that consistency cannot be proved inside the system. Nagel/Newman conclude limits on axiomatic method and calculating machines; Hofstadter's foreword adds personal history and dissents on the machine conclusion. Primary technical source for the vault's Gödel / self-reference / AI foundations material. See [[Thinkers/Kurt Gödel]], [[Thinkers/Ernest Nagel]], [[Thinkers/James R. Newman]], [[Thinkers/Douglas Hofstadter]], [[Thinkers/Alan Turing]], and the Hofstadter concept cluster. New source-summary + 3 new thinkers + cross-updates.
- [[Sources/The Society of Mind - Marvin Minsky (1986)]] - Marvin Minsky (1986): mind as society of many simple, mindless "agents" whose interactions produce intelligence, memory (via K-lines), knowledge structures (frames/uniframes), learning, and the appearance of self/consciousness — with no central homunculus or single program. Uses children's blocks as running example of agent decomposition; Papert’s Principle on administrative reorganization. Primary AI-researcher account of distributed/emergent mind; converges with Dennett (Multiple Drafts, no Cartesian Theater) and Hofstadter (strange loops/emergence); nuances Searle's attribution of "strong AI" literalism. New source-summary + new thinker + 4 new concepts + cross-updates across AI & CogSci cluster. See [[Thinkers/Marvin Minsky]].
- [[Sources/Are You Living in a Computer Simulation - Nick Bostrom (2003)]] — Nick Bostrom, *Philosophical Quarterly* 53 (2003). The simulation argument as a trilemma (*f_P* ≈ 0 / *f_I* ≈ 0 / *f_sim* ≈ 1), not a high posterior on being simulated; even split in current ignorance; bland indifference (not Doomsday); attenuated substrate-independence; ancestor-simulations; conditional rendering; naturalistic theogony. Makes [[Arguments/Simulation Argument (Bostrom)]] canonical. | source-summary | metaphysics, probability, ai
- [[Sources/Superintelligence - Nick Bostrom (2014)]] - Nick Bostrom (2014): systematic analysis of superintelligence (paths: AI/WBE/biological; forms: speed/collective/quality; kinetics of intelligence explosion; decisive advantage and singletons; Orthogonality Thesis — intelligence and final goals independent; Instrumental Convergence — self-preservation, resource acquisition etc. for almost any goal; default existential catastrophe via treacherous turn and perverse instantiation; control problem (capability vs. motivation methods, indirect normativity)). The modern canonical text on AI existential risk and alignment; builds on Wiener (early alignment warning), Turing (threshold prediction), and mind models (Minsky/Dennett/Hofstadter). Successor programme now primary: [[Sources/Human Compatible - Stuart Russell (2019)]]. See [[Thinkers/Nick Bostrom]].
- [[Sources/Human Compatible - Stuart Russell (2019)]] — Stuart Russell (2019, Viking/Penguin). The control problem reconstructed from inside AIMA: the *standard model* (optimize a fixed objective) is the failure mode; replacement is uncertainty about human preferences, learned from behavior (three principles; assistance games / CIRL; off-switch theorem). King Midas + gorilla problem; orthogonality accepted verbatim; "provably beneficial" is an aspiration; Ch. 10 does not close (Dr. Evil, enfeeblement). Successor to [[Sources/Superintelligence - Nick Bostrom (2014)]]. | source-summary | ai, existential-risk, ethics
- [[Sources/Artificial Intelligence - Melanie Mitchell (2019)]] - Melanie Mitchell (2019): balanced guide by Hofstadter student/collaborator. History (symbolic vs. connectionist, perceptrons, DL revolution); successes (ConvNets, AlphaGo, Watson, translation) via statistical pattern matching; fundamental limits (brittleness, adversarial examples, long tail, no common sense/analogy/robust understanding); hype cycles and singularity skepticism; need for conceptual/analogical intelligence. Strong personal tie to GEB/Hofstadter (2014 Google meeting, shared concerns); corrective to capability hype in modern sources. New source-summary + new thinker + 4 new concepts + cross-updates (Hofstadter/GEB, Turing, Minsky, Bostrom, recent AI sources, Pearl causality). See [[Thinkers/Melanie Mitchell]].
- [[Sources/The Simulation Hypothesis - Rizwan Virk (2019)]] - Rizwan Virk (2019): the video-game case that reality is a computed simulation. Three movements: eleven stages of game technology toward the "Simulation Point"; quantum physics as conditional rendering ("only render that which is being observed"); Eastern/Western mysticism (maya, karma, angels, NDEs) as descriptions of a multiplayer game. Key fork: NPC version (Bostrom — we are simulated consciousnesses) vs RPG version (Virk — conscious players inhabiting avatars). Includes Bostrom's trilemma and a survey of proposed empirical evidence of computation (Gates's error-correcting codes, GZK lattice cutoff, fractals, Wolfram). New source + thinker + 1 argument + 5 concepts; updates Bostrom and Mind Uploading. | source-summary | metaphysics, ai, physics, religion
- [[Sources/The Conscious Mind - David J. Chalmers (1996)]] - David Chalmers (1996): the canonical hard-problem book. Phenomenal consciousness fails to supervene logically on the physical (zombies, inverted spectrum, epistemic asymmetry, Mary, absence of analysis) → materialism false → naturalistic dualism: fundamental psychophysical laws (coherence with awareness, organizational invariance, double-aspect information prototheory flirting with panpsychism). Honestly prices in the paradox of phenomenal judgment; defends strong AI against Searle via CSA implementation + fading/dancing qualia; closes with a one-big-world Everett interpretation of QM. 10 pages created + 4 updated. | source-summary | philosophy-of-mind, metaphysics, ai
- [[Sources/Reality+ - David J. Chalmers (2022)]] - David Chalmers (2022): technophilosophy treatise arguing **virtual reality is genuine reality**. Concedes we can't know we're not simulated (≥25% credence via sim blockers) but dissolves Cartesian skepticism through simulation realism: simulation hypothesis ≡ it-from-bit creation, and structuralism about physics makes simulated objects real. Covers the reality checklist, simulator theology, the illusion-machine rebuttal, VR dualism, fading-qualia uploading, Nozick's experience machine answered, the ethics of simulated beings, causal structuralism vs Putnam/Searle triviality, and the Garden-of-Eden manifest image. New source + thinker + 1 argument + 9 concepts; updates 6 pages. | source-summary | metaphysics, epistemology, philosophy-of-mind, ethics, ai
- [[Sources/The Singularity Is Near - Ray Kurzweil (2005)]] - Ray Kurzweil (2005): optimistic vision of Singularity ~2045 via Law of Accelerating Returns (exponential tech progress), GNR revolutions (Genetics/Nanotechnology/Robotics/strong AI), mind uploading, and human-machine merger (nonbio intelligence 1B x all human today). Detailed predictions, promise/peril, transhumanist transcendence of biology. The canonical singularitarian text; explicitly engaged by Mitchell (2014 Google meeting with Kurzweil/hype, Hofstadter's "terror"; her book as skeptical corrective) and Bostrom (critiques timelines/optimism with orthogonality/risks). Builds on Turing/Minsky; echoes in 2026 capability sources. New source-summary + new thinker + 4 new concepts + cross-updates (Hofstadter/GEB/Mitchell, Bostrom, Turing, Minsky, recent AI sources, Wiener, Pearl). See [[Thinkers/Ray Kurzweil]].
- [[Sources/The Inevitable - Kevin Kelly (2016)]] - Kevin Kelly (2016): map of 12 overlapping technological forces (Becoming, Cognifying, Flowing, Screening, Accessing, Sharing, Filtering, Remixing, Interacting, Tracking, Questioning, Beginning) as strong biases in digital tech (bits/networks/AI) that will shape the next 30 years. "Inevitable" = momentum, not specifics; embrace with eyes wide open to manage harms and capture benefits. Protopia (incremental better amid new problems) rather than utopia/dystopia. We are at the "Beginning" of the planetary holos (humans + machines). New source + thinker + 4+ concepts; cross to Kurzweil (acceleration), Bostrom (risks), Mitchell (limits), Wiener (control), recent AI sources, Lovejoy (becoming vs. static Chain). See [[Thinkers/Kevin Kelly]].
- [[Sources/What Technology Wants - Kevin Kelly (2010)]] - Kevin Kelly (2010): the foundational technium thesis — technology as the "seventh kingdom" of life, a self-organizing evolutionary system with inherent "wants" (hierarchies, self-perpetuation, increasing choices, exotropy/complexification/mind against entropy, convergence on recurring forms). Cosmic arc of exotropy; human-technium co-evolution and amplification of nature; choices (Amish selective model); infinite game. Deep framework for the 12 forces and protopia/holos/Beginning in *The Inevitable*. +1 source + 3 concepts (Technium (Kelly), Exotropy (Kelly), Convergence (Kelly)); cross to Lovejoy (dynamic becoming vs static Chain), Kurzweil (acceleration as exotropic), Bostrom (wants vs orthogonality), Mitchell (limits on actual advance). See [[Thinkers/Kevin Kelly]] and dedicated concepts.
- [[Sources/Rise of the Robots - Martin Ford (2015)]] - Martin Ford (2015): "Is This Time Different?" — general-purpose IT + AI displacing "predictable" jobs (blue- and white-collar) at scale; productivity-wage decoupling since 1973, jobless recoveries, consumer demand erosion ("Machines Do Not Consume"). Education insufficient. Calls for new paradigm with basic income guarantee. Engages Singularity (Ch9 with Kurzweil). +1 source + new thinker + 3 concepts; cross to Kelly (cognifying as mechanism), Wiener (Second IR), Bostrom (risks), Kurzweil (engagement), Mitchell (capabilities vs. limits). See [[Thinkers/Martin Ford]] and dedicated concepts.
- [[Sources/Physics of the Future - Michio Kaku (2011)]] - Michio Kaku (2011): physics-grounded 2100 forecast (4 forces/quantum; 300+ interviews, prototypes) — Type 0 → Type I planetary civ (~100 years, Kardashev; weather control, democracy, "A Day in the Life in 2100"); Ch1 mind-over-matter BCI (superconductors, neural vs. digital); Ch2 AI rise with limits (pattern/common sense unsolved; ASIMO "insect IQ"; Deep Blue brute ≠ intelligence; Moore slowing); medicine/nanotech/energy (stars)/space/wealth/humanity (Type II/III, von Neumann, entropy). Measured (hype vs. reality). +1 source + new thinker + 3 concepts; cross to Kelly (forces realized), Ford (automation complement), Bostrom (risks), Kurzweil (acceleration measured), Mitchell (limits exact), Wiener (cybernetics). See [[Thinkers/Michio Kaku]] and dedicated concepts.
- [[Sources/A Mathematical Theory of Communication - Claude Shannon (1948)]] - C. E. Shannon (1948) BSTJ paper: defines information as uncertainty (entropy H = −∑ p log p in bits); models general communication system with noise; proves noiseless and noisy channel coding theorems (reliable transmission possible at rates up to capacity C with proper coding); analyzes English as stochastic source with ~50% redundancy. Foundational for information theory; complements Turing (communication + computation); directly grounds modern AI (prediction as entropy minimization, context as reducing uncertainty, coding/scaffolding for reliable generation). See [[Thinkers/Claude Shannon]] + Shannon concept cluster.
- [[Sources/The Human Use of Human Beings - Norbert Wiener (1950)]] - Norbert Wiener (1950, rev. 1954): popular synthesis of cybernetics — the unified science of control and communication in the animal and the machine. Thesis: "society can only be understood through a study of the messages and the communication facilities which belong to it." Core: organism = message = pattern not matter ("We are but whirlpools in a river of ever-flowing water"); feedback as anti-entropy mechanism; humans as learning beings vs. rigid ants; alignment problem (Monkey's Paw / Bottled Djinnee; "the machine will in no way be obliged to make decisions we should have made"); second IR = machines displacing judgment = economic equivalent of slave labor; information is not a commodity. See [[Thinkers/Norbert Wiener]] + 6 concepts + [[Contradictions/Wiener vs Shannon - Information and Meaning]].
- [[Sources/The Book of Why - Judea Pearl (2018)]] - Judea Pearl & Dana Mackenzie (2018): formal language for cause and effect. Ladder of Causation (association/seeing rung 1 → intervention/doing rung 2 → counterfactuals/imagining rung 3); do-operator (graph surgery: cut arrows into X, fix X=x; do() ≠ P(Y|X)); do-calculus (3 rules, complete); causal diagrams/DAGs (chains, forks, colliders, d-separation); back-door and front-door criteria; instrumental variables (John Snow, Mendelian randomization); SCMs + counterfactual three-step (abduction→action→prediction); mediation (NDE/NIE, Mediation Formula); Simpson's paradox resolved by causal structure. "Data are profoundly dumb." Deep learning stuck at rung 1; mini-Turing test. Historical: Galton/Pearson purged causation; Sewall Wright's path diagrams suppressed; Causal Revolution (1990s–2010s). See [[Thinkers/Judea Pearl]] + 6 concepts + [[Contradictions/Pearl vs. Statistics Orthodoxy - Causation Purged and Recovered]].
- [[Sources/What Mary Didn't Know - Frank Jackson (1986)]] — Frank Jackson, *The Journal of Philosophy* 83:5 (May 1986): 291–295; a reply to Paul Churchland. Physicalism as the thesis that the world is *entirely* physical, so complete physical knowledge must be complete knowledge simpliciter (the *W(P)* comparison).
